## [PART X]{.HLabel1}[Sanitation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-908625 .Part}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2024-118, s. 11(F)

\]
:::

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-908626 .Subheading}

- **[[10.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this Part,

  [[ARI]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ARI]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Air-Conditioning and
      Refrigeration Institute of the United States;
      ([ARI]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[change room]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[change room]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a room that is used by
      employees to change from their street clothes to their work
      clothes and from their work clothes to their street clothes, and
      includes a locker room; ([vestiaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[personal service room]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[personal service room]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a change room,
      toilet room, shower room, field accommodation or a combination
      thereof. ([local réservé aux soins personnels]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Section 1.6 does not apply in respect of any
  publication that is referred to in a standard that is incorporated by
  reference in this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-118, s. 12](#1455634-1498760){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1455634-1498760.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.1-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-908631 .Subheading}

- **[[10.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every employer shall maintain each
  personal service room and food preparation area used by employees in a
  clean and sanitary condition.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Personal service rooms and food preparation areas
  shall be so used by employees that the rooms or areas remain in as
  clean and sanitary a condition as is possible.

**[[10.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.3 .sectionLabel}** All cleaning and
sweeping that may cause dusty or unsanitary conditions shall be carried
out in a manner that prevents the contamination of the air by dust or
other substances injurious to health.

**[[10.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.4 .sectionLabel}** Each personal service
room shall be cleaned at least once every day that it is used.

**[[10.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.5 .sectionLabel}** Every plumbing system
that supplies potable water and removes water-borne waste shall be
installed and maintained by a qualified person.

- **[[10.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Each enclosed part of a work place,
  each personal service room and each food preparation area shall be
  constructed, equipped and maintained in a manner that prevents the
  entrance of vermin.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where vermin have entered any enclosed part of a work
  place, any personal service room or any food preparation area, the
  employer shall immediately take all steps necessary to eliminate the
  vermin and prevent the re-entry of the vermin.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-165, s. 27(E)
:::

**[[10.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.7 .sectionLabel}** No person shall use a
personal service room for the purpose of storing equipment unless a
closet fitted with a door is provided in that room for that purpose.

**[[10.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.8 .sectionLabel}** In each personal
service room and food preparation area, the temperature, measured 1 m
above the floor in the centre of the room or area, shall be maintained
at a level of not less than 18°C and, where reasonably practicable, not
more than 29°C.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-165, s. 28(F)
:::

- **[[10.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  each personal service room and food preparation area, the floors,
  partitions and walls shall be so constructed that they can be easily
  washed and maintained in a sanitary condition.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The floor and lower 150 mm of any walls and
  partitions in any food preparation area or toilet room shall be
  water-tight and impervious to moisture.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-165, s. 29(E)
:::

### [Toilet Rooms]{.HTitleText2} {#h-908652 .Subheading}

- **[[10.10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.10
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where reasonably practicable, a
  toilet room shall be provided for employees and, where persons of both
  sexes are employed at the same work place, a separate toilet room
  shall be provided for employees of each sex.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where separate toilet rooms are provided for
  employees of each sex, each room shall be equipped with a door that is
  clearly marked to indicate the sex of the employees for whom the room
  is provided.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where persons of both sexes use the same toilet room,
  the door of the toilet room shall be fitted on the inside with a
  locking device.

<!-- -->

- **[[10.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every toilet room shall be so
  designed that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is completely enclosed with solid material that
    is non-transparent from the outside;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), there is no direct
    access into the toilet room from a sleeping room, dining area or
    food preparation area;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} where reasonably practicable, there is direct
    access into the toilet room from a hallway; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if it contains more than one water closet, each
    water closet is enclosed in a separate compartment fitted with a
    door and an inside locking device.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a toilet is provided as part of private field
  accommodation, there may be direct access thereto from the sleeping
  quarters for which the toilet room is provided.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-165, s. 30(F)
:::

**[[10.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.12 .sectionLabel}** Toilet paper shall
be provided at each water closet.

- **[[10.13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.13
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The employer shall provide menstrual
  products, including clean and hygienic tampons and menstrual pads, in
  each toilet room.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If it is not feasible to provide menstrual products
  in a toilet room, the employer shall provide them in another location
  in the same work place that is controlled by the employer, accessible
  by employees at all times and offers a reasonable amount of privacy.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The employer shall provide a covered container for
  the disposal of menstrual products

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in any toilet room that has only one toilet; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in each toilet compartment of any toilet room that
    has more than one toilet.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-78, s. 3](#1397690-1430607){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1397690-1430607.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.13-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.13"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Wash Basins]{.HTitleText2} {#h-908668 .Subheading}

- **[[10.14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.14
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every employer shall provide wash
  basins in each toilet room as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where the room contains one or two water closets or
    urinals, one wash basin; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the room contains more than two water closets
    or urinals, one wash basin for every additional two water closets or
    urinals.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where an outdoor privy is provided, the employer
  shall provide wash basins required by subsection (1) as close to the
  outdoor privy as is reasonably practicable.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An industrial wash trough or circular wash basin of a
  capacity equivalent to the aggregate of the minimum capacities of the
  wash basins referred to in subsection (1) may be provided in place of
  the wash basins.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (3), the minimum
  capacity of a wash basin shall be determined by reference to the
  applicable municipal by-laws or provincial regulations or, if there
  are no such by-laws or regulations, by reference to the National
  Plumbing Code of Canada 2020, developed by the Canadian Commission on
  Building and Fire Codes and published by the National Research Council
  of Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-165, s. 31
- [SOR/2024-118, s. 13](#1455636-1498762){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1455636-1498762.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.14-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.14"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[10.15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.15 .sectionLabel}** All wash basins and
industrial wash troughs and circular wash basins referred to in section
10.14 shall be supplied with hot and cold water.

**[[10.16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.16 .sectionLabel}** Where the health of
employees is likely to be endangered by skin contact with a hazardous
substance, the employer shall provide wash facilities to clean the skin
and aid in the removal of the hazardous substance.

::: HistoricalNote
- SORS/88-199, s. 19
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.16-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.16"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[10.17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.17 .sectionLabel}** In every personal
service room that contains a wash basin or an industrial wash trough or
circular wash basin, the employer shall provide

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} powdered or liquid soap or other cleaning agent in a
  dispenser at each wash basin or trough or between adjoining wash
  basins;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} sufficient sanitary hand drying facilities to serve
  the number of employees using the personal service room; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a non-combustible container for the disposal of used
  towels where disposable towels are provided for drying hands.

### [Showers and Shower Rooms]{.HTitleText2} {#h-908686 .Subheading}

- **[[10.18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.18
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A shower room with at least one
  shower head for every 10 employees or portion of that number shall be
  provided for employees who regularly perform strenuous physical work
  in a high temperature or high humidity or whose bodies may be
  contaminated by a hazardous substance.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every shower stall shall be constructed and arranged
  in such a way that water cannot leak through the walls or floors.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every shower shall be provided with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} hot and cold water;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} soap or other cleaning agent; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a clean towel.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where duck boards are used in showers, they shall not
  be made of wood.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 19
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.18-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.18"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Potable Water]{.HTitleText2} {#h-908697 .Subheading}

**[[10.19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.19 .sectionLabel}** Every employer
shall provide potable water for drinking, personal washing and food
preparation that conforms to the Guidelines for Canadian Drinking Water
Quality, published by the Department of Health, as amended from time to
time.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-118, s. 14](#1455638-1498765){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1455638-1498765.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.19-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.19"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[10.20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.20 .sectionLabel}** Where water is
transported for drinking, personal washing or food preparation, only
sanitary water containers shall be used.

**[[10.21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.21 .sectionLabel}** Where a storage
container for drinking water is used,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the container shall be securely covered and labelled
  that it contains potable water;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the container shall be used only for the purpose of
  storing potable water; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the water shall be drawn from the container by

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a tap,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a ladle used only for the purpose of drawing water
    from the container, or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any other means that precludes the contamination
    of the water.

**[[10.22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.22 .sectionLabel}** Except where
drinking water is supplied by a drinking fountain, sanitary single-use
drinking cups shall be provided.

**[[10.23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.23 .sectionLabel}** Any ice that is
added to drinking water or used for the contact refrigeration of
foodstuffs shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} be made from potable water; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} be so stored and handled as to prevent contamination.

**[[10.24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.24 .sectionLabel}** A drinking fountain
that supplies drinking water shall meet the standards set out in the ARI
standard ARI 1010-2002, entitled Self-Contained,
Mechanically-Refrigerated Drinking-Water Coolers, as amended from time
to time.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-118, s. 15](#1455640-1498768){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1455640-1498768.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.24-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.24"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Field Accommodation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-908712 .Subheading}

**[[10.25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.25 .sectionLabel}** All field
accommodation shall meet the following standards:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} it shall be so constructed that it can easily be
  cleaned and disinfected;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the food preparation area and dining area shall be
  separated from the sleeping quarters;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} where a water plumbing system is provided, the system
  shall operate under sanitary conditions;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} garbage disposal facilities shall be provided to
  prevent the accumulation of garbage;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} toilet rooms and outdoor privies shall be maintained
  in a sanitary condition; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} vermin prevention, heating, ventilation and sanitary
  sewage systems shall be provided.

<!-- -->

- **[[10.26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.26
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-118, s.
  16\]]{.Repealed}

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The installation of plumbing fixtures in field
  accommodation shall meet the standards set out in the National
  Plumbing Code of Canada 2020, developed by the Canadian Commission on
  Building and Fire Codes and published by the National Research Council
  of Canada.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In field accommodation that is not equipped with
  sleeping quarters, the employer shall provide water closets and wash
  basins in accordance with subsection 3.6.4 of the National Building
  Code.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Every mobile accommodation that is provided as field
  accommodation shall meet the standards set out in chapter Z240.2.1-16,
  entitled Technical requirements for manufactured homes, of the CSA
  standard Z240 MH Series-16, entitled Manufactured Homes, as amended
  from time to time.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of clause 4.12.4 of the Standard
  referred to in subsection (4), there is no other approved method.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-165, s. 32(F)
- [SOR/2022-94, s. 11(F)](#1343840-1344486){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1343840-1344486.html"}
- [SOR/2024-118, s. 16](#1455642-1498773){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1455642-1498773.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.26-20220502.html "Link to previous version of section 10.26"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Sleeping Quarters]{.HTitleText2} {#h-908728 .Subheading}

**[[10.27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.27 .sectionLabel}** In any field
accommodation provided as sleeping quarters for employees

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a separate bed or bunk that is not part of a unit
  that is more than double-tiered and is so constructed that it can be
  easily cleaned and disinfected shall be provided for each employee;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} mattresses, pillows, sheets, pillow cases, blankets,
  bed covers and sleeping bags shall be kept in a clean and sanitary
  condition; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a storage area fitted with a locking device shall be
  provided for each employee.

### [Preparation, Handling, Storage and Serving of Food]{.HTitleText2} {#h-908733 .Subheading}

- **[[10.28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.28
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Each food handler shall be
  instructed and trained in food handling practices that prevent the
  contamination of food.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person who is suffering from a communicable
  disease shall work as a food handler.

**[[10.29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.29 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2024-118, s. 17\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.29-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.29"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[10.30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.30
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Foods that require refrigeration to
  prevent them from becoming hazardous to health shall be maintained at
  a temperature of 4°C or lower.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Foods that require freezing shall be maintained at a
  temperature of -11°C or lower.

**[[10.31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.31 .sectionLabel}** All equipment and
utensils that come into contact with food shall be

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} designed to be easily cleaned;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} smooth and free from cracks, crevices, pitting or
  unnecessary indentations; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} cleaned and stored to maintain their surfaces in a
  sanitary condition.

**[[10.32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.32 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
eat, prepare or store food

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in an area where a hazardous substance may
  contaminate food, dishes or utensils;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in a personal service room that contains a water
  closet, urinal or shower; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other area where food is likely to be
  contaminated.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 19
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.32-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.32"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Food Waste and Garbage]{.HTitleText2} {#h-908751 .Subheading}

- **[[10.33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.33
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Food waste and garbage shall be
  removed daily from personal service rooms and food preparation areas.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Food waste and garbage shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} disposed of by a sanitary drainage system;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} held in a garbage container; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} incinerated.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every employer shall adopt and implement a procedure
  that requires that combustible garbage not be incinerated unless
  precautions have been taken to ensure that the fire does not endanger
  employees, the safety of the work place or the integrity of any
  equipment.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-165, s. 33(E)
:::

**[[10.34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.34 .sectionLabel}** Garbage containers
shall be

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} maintained in a clean and sanitary condition;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} cleaned and disinfected in an area separate from
  personal service rooms and food preparation areas;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} where there may be internal pressure in the
  container, so designed that the pressure is relieved by controlled
  ventilation;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} constructed of a non-absorbent material and provided
  with a tight-fitting top;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} located in an area that is inaccessible to animals;
  and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} where liquids, wet materials or food waste are
  disposed of therein, leakproof.

### [Dining Areas]{.HTitleText2} {#h-908768 .Subheading}

**[[10.35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.35 .sectionLabel}** Every dining area
provided by the employer shall be

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} of sufficient size to allow seating and table space
  for the employees who normally use the dining area at any one time;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} provided with non-combustible covered receptacles for
  the disposal of food waste or garbage; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} separated from any place where a hazardous substance
  may contaminate food, dishes or utensils.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 19
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.35-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.35"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Ventilation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-908775 .Subheading}

**[[10.36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.36 .sectionLabel}** The intake or
exhaust duct for a ventilation system shall be so located that no
employee may be exposed to any hazardous substance drawn in or exhausted
through the duct.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-165, s. 34
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.36-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.36"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Clothing Storage]{.HTitleText2} {#h-908779 .Subheading}

**[[10.37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.37 .sectionLabel}** Clothing storage
facilities shall be provided by the employer for the storage of
overcoats and other clothes not worn by employees while they are
working.

- **[[10.38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.38
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A change room shall be provided by
  the employer where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the nature of the work engaged in by an employee
    makes it necessary for the employee to change from street clothes to
    work clothes for safety or health reasons; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an employee is regularly engaged in work in which
    his work clothing becomes wet or contaminated by a hazardous
    substance.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where wet or contaminated work clothing referred to
  in paragraph (1)(b) is changed, it shall be stored in such a manner
  that it does not come in contact with clothing that is not wet or
  contaminated.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No employee shall leave the work place wearing
  clothing contaminated by a hazardous substance.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Every employer shall supply facilities for the drying
  or cleaning of wet or contaminated clothing referred to in paragraph
  (1)(b).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 19
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.38-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.38"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART XI]{.HLabel1}[Hazardous Substances]{.HTitleText1} {#h-908790 .Part}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/88-199, s. 19
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)

\]
:::

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-908793 .Subheading}

**[[11.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.1 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,

[[airborne asbestos fibres]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[airborne asbestos fibres]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means asbestos
    fibres that are longer than 5 μm (micrometres) with an aspect ratio
    equal to or greater than 3:1 and that are carried by the
    air; ([fibres d'amiante aéroportées]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[asbestos]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[asbestos]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means actinolite, amosite,
    anthophyllite, chrysotile, crocidolite and tremolite in their
    fibrous form; ([amiante]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[asbestos-containing material]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[asbestos-containing material]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any article that is manufactured and contains 1%
      or more asbestos by weight at the time of manufacture or that
      contains a concentration of 1% or more asbestos as determined in
      accordance with Method 9002 set out in the document entitled NIOSH
      Manual of Analytical Methods, published by the National Institute
      for Occupational Safety and Health, as amended from time to time,
      or in accordance with a scientifically proven method used to
      collect and analyze a representative sample of the material, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any material that contains a concentration of 1%
      or more asbestos as determined in accordance with Method 9002 set
      out in the document entitled NIOSH Manual of Analytical Methods,
      published by the National Institute for Occupational Safety and
      Health, as amended from time to time, or in accordance with a
      scientifically proven method used to collect and analyze a
      representative sample of the material; ([matériau contenant de
      l'amiante]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[clearance air sampling]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[clearance air sampling]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the action of
    taking samples to determine if the concentration of airborne
    asbestos fibres inside an enclosure is below the limit referred to
    in section 11.23 to permit the dismantling of a containment
    system; ([échantillonnage de l'air après
    décontamination]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[containment system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[containment system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an isolation system
    that is designed to effectively contain asbestos fibre within a
    designated work area where asbestos-containing material is handled,
    removed, encapsulated or enclosed; ([système de
    confinement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[encapsulation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[encapsulation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the treatment of an
    asbestos-containing material with a sealant that penetrates the
    material and binds the asbestos fibres together, and the treatment
    of the surface of the asbestos-containing material with a sealant
    that creates a membrane on the surface, to prevent the release of
    asbestos fibres into the air; ([encapsulation]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[enclosure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[enclosure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a physical barrier such as
    drywall, plywood or metal sheeting that, as part of the containment
    system, isolates asbestos-containing material from adjacent areas in
    a building to prevent the release of airborne asbestos fibres into
    those areas; ([encloisonnement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[friable]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[friable]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of
    asbestos-containing material, that the material, when dry, can be
    easily crumbled or powdered by hand
    pressure; ([friable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[glove bag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[glove bag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a polyethylene or polyvinyl
    chloride bag that is affixed around an asbestos-containing source
    and that permits asbestos-containing material to be removed while
    minimizing the release of airborne asbestos fibres into the work
    place; ([sac à gants]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[hazard information]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[hazard information]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a
    hazardous substance, information on the proper and safe storage,
    handling, use and disposal of the hazardous substance, including
    information relating to the health and physical hazards that it
    presents; ([renseignements sur les risques]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[HEPA filter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[HEPA filter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a high-efficiency
    particulate air filter that has been tested to ensure efficiency
    equal to or exceeding 99.97% for removal of airborne particles
    having a mean aerodynamic diameter of 0.3 µm (micrometres) from the
    air; ([filtre HEPA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[high-risk activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[high-risk activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an activity that
    involves the handling or disturbance of friable asbestos-containing
    material or is carried out in proximity to friable
    asbestos-containing material, that requires a high level of control
    to prevent exposure to excessive concentrations of airborne asbestos
    fibres and that includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the removal or disturbance of more than 1 m^2^ of
      friable asbestos-containing material in a work place, even if the
      activity is divided into smaller jobs,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the spray application of a sealant to a friable
      asbestos-containing material,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the cleaning or removal of air-handling
      equipment, other than filters, in a building that has sprayed-on
      fireproofing or sprayed-on thermal insulation that is
      asbestos-containing material,

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the repair, alteration or demolition of all or
      part of a kiln, metallurgical furnace or similar structure that
      contains asbestos-containing material,

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the breaking, cutting, drilling, abrading,
      grinding, sanding or vibrating of non-friable asbestos-containing
      material, if the activity is carried out by means of power tools
      that are not attached to dust-collecting devices equipped with
      HEPA filters, and

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the repair, alteration or demolition of all or
      part of a building in which asbestos is or was used in the
      manufacture of products, unless the asbestos was cleaned up and
      removed; ([activité à risque élevé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[lower explosive limit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[lower explosive limit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the lower limit
    of flammability of a chemical agent or a combination of chemical
    agents at ambient temperature and pressure, expressed

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a gas or vapour, as a percentage per volume
      of air, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for dust, as the weight of dust per volume of
      air; ([limite explosive inférieure]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[low-risk activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[low-risk activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an activity that
    involves the handling of asbestos-containing material or is carried
    out in proximity to non-friable asbestos-containing material and
    that includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the installation or removal of ceiling tiles that
      are made of non-friable asbestos-containing material and cover an
      area of less than 7.5 m^2^,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the installation or removal of other non-friable
      asbestos-containing material, if the material is not being broken,
      cut, drilled, abraded, ground, sanded or vibrated and dust is not
      being generated,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the breaking, cutting, drilling, abrading,
      grinding, sanding or vibrating of non-friable asbestos-containing
      material, if the material is wetted to control the spread of dust
      or fibres and the activity is carried out only by means of
      non-powered hand-held tools, and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the removal of less than 1 m² of drywall in which
      joint cement containing asbestos has been used; ([activité à
      faible risque]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[moderate-risk activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[moderate-risk activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an activity
    that involves the handling of asbestos-containing material or is
    carried out in proximity to friable asbestos-containing material,
    that is not otherwise classified as a low-risk activity or high-risk
    activity and that includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the removal of all or part of a false ceiling to
      gain access to a work area, if asbestos-containing material is
      likely to be found on the surface of the false ceiling,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the removal or disturbance of 1 m^2^ or less of
      friable asbestos-containing material during repair, alteration,
      maintenance or demolition work in a work place,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the enclosure of friable asbestos-containing
      material,

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the application of tape, sealant or other
      covering to pipe or boiler insulation that is asbestos-containing
      material,

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the removal of ceiling tiles that are
      asbestos-containing material, if the tiles cover an area of
      greater than 2 m^2^ and are removed without being broken, cut,
      drilled, abraded, ground, sanded or vibrated,

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the breaking, cutting, drilling, abrading,
      grinding, sanding or vibrating of non-friable asbestos-containing
      material, if the material is not wetted to control the spread of
      dust or fibres and the activity is carried out only by means of
      non-powered hand-held tools,

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the removal of 1 m^2^ or more of drywall in which
      joint cement that is asbestos-containing material has been used,

    - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the breaking, cutting, drilling, abrading,
      grinding, sanding or vibrating of non-friable asbestos-containing
      material, if the activity is carried out by means of power tools
      that are attached to dust-collecting devices equipped with HEPA
      filters,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the removal of insulation that is
      asbestos-containing material from a pipe, duct or similar
      structure using a glove bag, and

    - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the cleaning or removal of filters used in
      air-handling equipment in a building that has sprayed-on
      fireproofing that is asbestos-containing material; ([activité à
      risque modéré]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[product identifier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[product identifier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
    in subsection 1(1) of the [Hazardous Products
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2015-17); ([identificateur de
    produit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[readily available]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[readily available]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/2025-79, s. 20\]]{.Repealed}

[[supplier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[supplier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in section
    2 of the [Hazardous Products
    Act](/eng/acts/H-3); ([fournisseur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[work activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[work activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any low-risk activity,
    moderate-risk activity or high-risk activity or any activity that is
    ancillary to that activity, and the supervision of that activity and
    that ancillary activity. ([activité de travail]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 4
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
- SOR/2016-141, s. 35
- SOR/2017-132, s. 9
- [SOR/2025-79, s. 20](#1520602-1525060){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1520602-1525060.html"}
:::

Version](section-11.1-20170620.html "Link to previous version of section 11.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-908840 .Subheading}

**[[11.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.2 .sectionLabel}** This Part does not
apply to the transportation or handling of dangerous goods to which the
Transportation of Dangerous Goods Act and regulations made thereunder
apply.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 5
:::

### [DIVISION I]{.HLabel2}[General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-908844 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/88-199, s. 5

\]
:::

#### [Hazard Investigation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-908847 .Subheading}

- **[[11.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  there is a likelihood that the safety or health of an employee in a
  work place is or may be endangered by exposure to a hazardous
  substance, by insufficient lighting or excessive sound level, the
  employer shall, without delay,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} appoint a qualified person to carry out an
    investigation; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} notify the work place committee or the health and
    safety representative, if either exists, of the proposed
    investigation and of the name of the qualified person appointed to
    carry out that investigation.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the investigation referred to in subsection (1),
  the following criteria shall be taken into consideration:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the chemical, biological and physical properties of
    the hazardous substance;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the routes of exposure to the hazardous substance;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the effects to safety and health caused by exposure
    to the hazardous substance;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the state, concentration and quantity of the
    hazardous substance handled;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the manner in which the hazardous substance is
    handled;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the control methods used to eliminate or reduce
    exposure to the hazardous substance;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the possibility that the concentration of the
    hazardous substance to which an employee is likely to be exposed
    exceeds the limit referred to in section 11.23;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the possibility that the level of lighting in the
    work place is less than the level prescribed in Part VII; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the possibility that the level of sound in the work
    place is greater than the level prescribed in Part VIII.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 19
- SOR/94-165, s. 35
- SOR/2014-141, s. 3
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.3-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 11.3"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[11.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.4 .sectionLabel}** On completion of the
investigation referred to in subsection 11.3(1) and after consultation
with the safety and health committee or the safety and health
representative, if either exists, the qualified person shall set out in
a written report signed by the qualified person

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} his observations respecting the criteria considered
  in accordance with subsection 11.3(2); and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} his recommendations respecting the manner of
  compliance with sections 11.6 to 11.28.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-165, s. 36
:::

**[[11.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.5 .sectionLabel}** The report referred
to in section 11.4 shall be kept by the employer at the work place to
which it applies for one year after the date on which the qualified
person signed the report.

#### [Substitution of Substances]{.HTitleText3} {#h-908870 .Subheading}

- **[[11.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  hazardous substance shall not be used for any purpose in a work place
  if it is reasonably practicable to substitute therefor a substance
  that is not a hazardous substance.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a hazardous substance is required to be used
  for any purpose in a work place and an equivalent substance that is
  less hazardous is available to be used for that purpose, the
  equivalent substance shall be substituted for the hazardous substance
  where reasonably practicable.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 19
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.6-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 11.6"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Ventilation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-908876 .Subheading}

**[[11.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.7 .sectionLabel}** Every ventilation
system used to control the concentration of an airborne hazardous
substance shall be so designed, constructed and installed that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the substance is a chemical agent, the
  concentration of the chemical agent does not exceed the limits
  prescribed in sections 11.23 and 11.24; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the substance is not a chemical agent, the
  concentration of the substance is not hazardous to the safety or
  health of employees.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 19
- SOR/2014-141, s. 4
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.7-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 11.7"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Air Pressure]{.HTitleText3} {#h-908882 .Subheading}

- **[[11.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.8
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), where
  there is a likelihood that explosive or toxic vapours may enter an
  enclosed work place or field accommodation, the air pressure in the
  work place or field accommodation shall, where practicable, be
  maintained positive in relation to the air pressure in the surrounding
  area.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where there is a source of explosive or toxic vapours
  at a work place, the air pressure in the area of the source shall be
  maintained negative with respect to any adjacent enclosed area.

#### [Warnings]{.HTitleText3} {#h-908886 .Subheading}

**[[11.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.9 .sectionLabel}** Where reasonably
practicable, automated warning and detection systems shall be provided
by the employer where the seriousness of any exposure to a hazardous
substance so requires.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 19
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.9-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 11.9"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Storage, Handling and Use]{.HTitleText3} {#h-908890 .Subheading}

**[[11.10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.10 .sectionLabel}** Every hazardous
substance stored, handled or used in a work place shall be stored,
handled and used in a manner whereby the hazard related to that
substance is reduced to a minimum.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 19
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.10-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 11.10"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[11.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.11 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section
11.14, where a hazardous substance is stored, handled or used in a work
place, any hazard resulting from that storage, handling or use shall be
confined to as small an area as practicable.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 19
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.11-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 11.11"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[11.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.12
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every container for a hazardous
  substance that is used in a work place shall be so designed and
  constructed that it protects the employees from any safety or health
  hazard that is created by the hazardous substance.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a container referred to in subsection (1) is
  emptied and is not to be refilled with the hazardous substance, it
  shall be completely cleaned of the hazardous substance that was stored
  in it before being reused and the label identifying the hazardous
  substance shall be removed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, ss. 6, 19
- SOR/94-165, s. 37
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.12-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 11.12"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[11.13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.13 .sectionLabel}** The quantity of a
hazardous substance used or processed in a work place shall, as far as
is practicable, be kept to a minimum.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 19
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.13-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 11.13"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[11.14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.14 .sectionLabel}** Where a hazardous
substance is capable of combining with another substance to form an
ignitable combination and a hazard of ignition of the combination by
static electricity exists, the standards set out in the United States
*National Fire Prevention Association Inc.* publication NFPA 77-1983,
Recommended Practice on Static Electricity, dated 1983, shall be adopted
and implemented.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 19
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.14-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 11.14"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Warning of Hazardous Substances]{.HTitleText3} {#h-908908 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/88-199, s. 7
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)

\]
:::

- **[[11.15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.15
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where a hazardous substance is
  stored in a work place, signs shall be posted in conspicuous places
  warning of the presence of the hazardous substance.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Hazard information in respect of hazardous substances
  that are, or are likely to be, present in a work place shall be
  readily available for examination by employees in any form, as
  determined in consultation with the policy committee or, if there is
  no policy committee, the safety and health committee or the safety and
  health representative.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 19
- SOR/94-165, s. 38
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
- SOR/2016-141, s. 36
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.15-20140529.html "Link to previous version of section 11.15"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[11.16 and 11.17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.16_and_11.17
.sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/88-199, s. 8\]]{.Repealed}

#### [Assembly of Pipes]{.HTitleText3} {#h-908917 .Subheading}

**[[11.18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.18 .sectionLabel}** Every assembly of
pipes, pipe fittings, valves, safety devices, pumps, compressors and
other fixed equipment that is used for transferring a hazardous
substance from one location to another shall be

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} marked, by labelling, colour-coding, placarding or
  any other mode, to identify the hazardous substance being transferred
  and, if appropriate, the direction of the flow;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} fitted with valves and other control and safety
  devices to ensure its safe operation;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} inspected by a qualified person before it is placed
  in service and once a year thereafter; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} maintained and repaired by a qualified person.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 9 and 19
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
- SOR/2016-141, s. 37
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.18-20140529.html "Link to previous version of section 11.18"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Employee Education and Training]{.HTitleText3} {#h-908925 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2016-141, s. 38(E)

\]
:::

- **[[11.19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.19
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every employer shall, in
  consultation with the policy committee or, if there is no policy
  committee, the safety and health committee or the safety and health
  representative, develop and implement an employee education and
  training program with respect to hazard prevention and control at the
  work place.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The employee education and training program shall
  include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the education and training of each employee who
    handles or is exposed to or who is likely to handle or be exposed to
    a hazardous substance, with respect to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the product identifier of the hazardous
      substance,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} all hazard information disclosed by the supplier
      or by the employer on a safety data sheet or label,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} all hazard information of which the employer is
      aware or ought to be aware,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the observations referred to in paragraph
      11.4(a),

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the information disclosed on a safety data sheet
      referred to in section 11.30 and the purpose and significance of
      that information,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} in respect of hazardous products in the work
      place, the information required to be disclosed on a safety data
      sheet and on a label under Division III and the purpose and
      significance of that information, and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the hazard information referred to in
      subsection 11.15(2);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the education and training of each employee who
    operates, maintains or repairs an assembly of pipes referred to in
    section 11.18, with respect to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} every valve and other control and safety device
      connected to the assembly of pipes,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the procedures to follow for the proper and safe
      use of the assembly of pipes, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the significance of the labelling,
      colour-coding, placarding or other modes of identification that
      are used;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the education and training of each employee who is
    referred to in paragraph (a) or (b), with respect to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the procedures to follow to implement sections
      11.10, 11.11 and 11.14,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the procedures to follow for the safe storage,
      handling, use and disposal of hazardous substances, including
      procedures to be followed in an emergency involving a hazardous
      substance, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the procedures to follow if an employee is
      exposed to fugitive emissions as defined in section 11.31; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the education and training of each employee on the
    procedures to follow to access electronic or paper versions of
    reports, records of education and training given and safety data
    sheets.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every employer shall, in consultation with the policy
  committee or, if there is no policy committee, the safety and health
  committee or the safety and health representative, review and, if
  necessary, revise the employee education and training program

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least once a year;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} whenever there is a change in conditions in respect
    of the presence of hazardous substances in the work place; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} whenever new hazard information in respect of a
    hazardous substance in the work place becomes available to the
    employer.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 10
- SOR/94-165, s. 39
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
- SOR/2016-141, s. 39
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.19-20140529.html "Link to previous version of section 11.19"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[11.20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.20 .sectionLabel}** The employer shall
keep a written record of the employee education and training program
referred to in subsection 11.19(1) readily available for examination by
employees in any form, as determined in consultation with the policy
committee or, if there is no policy committee, the safety and health
committee or the safety and health representative, for as long as the
employees

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} handle or are exposed to or are likely to handle or
  be exposed to the hazardous substance; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} operate, maintain or repair the assembly of pipes.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 10
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
- SOR/2016-141, s. 40
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.20-20140529.html "Link to previous version of section 11.20"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[11.21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.21 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/88-199, s. 10\]]{.Repealed}

#### [Medical Examinations]{.HTitleText3} {#h-908960 .Subheading}

- **[[11.22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.22
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where the report referred to in
  section 11.4 contains a recommendation for a medical examination, the
  employer may, regarding that recommendation, consult a physician who
  has specialized knowledge in respect of the hazardous substance in the
  work place.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the employer

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} consults a physician pursuant to subsection (1) and
    the physician confirms the recommendation for a medical examination,
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} does not consult a physician pursuant to subsection
    (1),

  the employer shall not permit an employee to work with the hazardous
  substance in the work place until a physician, who has the specialized
  knowledge referred to in subsection (1) and is acceptable to the
  employee, has examined the employee and declared the employee fit for
  work with the hazardous substance.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where an employer consults a physician pursuant to
  subsection (1), the employer shall keep a copy of the decision of the
  physician with the report referred to in section 11.4.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The cost of a medical examination referred to in
  subsection (2) shall be borne by the employer.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, ss. 11(E) and 19
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.22-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 11.22"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Control of Hazards]{.HTitleText3} {#h-908971 .Subheading}

- **[[11.23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.23
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No employee shall be exposed to a
  concentration of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an airborne chemical agent, other than grain dust
    or airborne asbestos fibres, in excess of the value for that
    chemical agent adopted by the American Conference of Governmental
    Industrial Hygienists in its publication entitled Threshold Limit
    Values (TLVs) and Biological Exposure Indices (BEIs), as amended
    from time to time;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} airborne grain dust, respirable and non-respirable,
    in excess of 10 mg/m^3^; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an airborne hazardous substance, other than a
    chemical agent, that is hazardous to the safety and health of the
    employee.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} An employer shall ensure that an employee's
  exposure to a concentration of airborne asbestos fibres is as close to
  zero as is reasonably practicable, but in any event the employer shall
  ensure that the concentration is not in excess of the value for
  airborne asbestos fibres adopted by the American Conference of
  Governmental Industrial Hygienists in its publication entitled
  Threshold Limit Values (TLVs) and Biological Exposure Indices (BEIs),
  as amended from time to time.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If there is a likelihood that the concentration of an
  airborne chemical agent may exceed any value referred to in paragraph
  (1)(a) or (b), the concentration of airborne asbestos fibres may
  exceed zero or there is a concentration of an airborne hazardous
  substance that is hazardous to the safety and health of the employee,
  the air shall be sampled by a qualified person and the concentration
  of the chemical agent, airborne asbestos fibres or hazardous substance
  determined by means of a test in accordance with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the standards set out by the United States National
    Institute for Occupational Safety and Health in the NIOSH Manual of
    Analytical Methods, Third Edition, Volumes 1 and 2, dated February
    1984; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a method set out in the United States Federal
    Register, Volume 40, Number 33, dated February 18, 1975, as amended
    by Volume 41, Number 53, dated March 17, 1976.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A record of each test made in accordance with
  subsection (2) shall be kept for two years after the date of the test
  by the employer at the employer's place of business that is nearest to
  the work place where the air was sampled.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The record referred to in subsection (3) shall
  include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date, time and location of the test;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the chemical agent for which the test was made;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the sampling and testing method used;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the result obtained; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the name and occupation of the qualified person who
    made the test.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 19
- SOR/94-165, s. 40(F)
- SOR/2014-141, s. 5
- SOR/2016-141, s. 41
- SOR/2017-132, s. 10
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.23-20160614.html "Link to previous version of section 11.23"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[11.24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.24
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (3),
  the concentration of an airborne chemical agent or combination of
  chemical agents in a work place shall be less than 50 per cent of the
  lower explosive limit of the chemical agent or combination of chemical
  agents.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a source of ignition may ignite an airborne
  chemical agent or combination of airborne chemical agents in a work
  place, the concentration of the chemical agent or the combination of
  chemical agents shall not exceed 10% of the lower explosive limit of
  the chemical agent or combination of chemical agents.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the work place is a Class I Division 1 or a Class I
    Division 2 area, as defined in section 18 of the Canadian Electrical
    Code;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the work place is equipped with an alarm system
    that will automatically be activated when the concentration referred
    to in subsection (1) exceeds 60 per cent of the lower explosive
    limit of the chemical agent or combination of chemical agents; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} no employee is exposed to a level in excess of 75
    per cent of the lower explosive limit of the chemical agent or
    combination of chemical agents.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-79, s. 21](#1520603-1525066){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1520603-1525066.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.24-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 11.24"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[11.25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.25
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Compressed air shall be used in such
  a manner that the air is not directed forcibly against any person.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The use of compressed air shall not result in a
  concentration of an airborne hazardous substance that is in excess of
  the values for the hazardous substance referred to in subsections
  11.23(1) or (1.1).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 19
- SOR/2014-141, s. 6
- SOR/2017-132, s. 11
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.25-20140529.html "Link to previous version of section 11.25"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Explosives]{.HTitleText3} {#h-909002 .Subheading}

- **[[11.26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.26
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No detonator shall be stored with an
  explosive that is not a detonator.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No detonator shall be stored with a detonator of a
  different type.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Not more than 75 kg of explosives shall be stored on
  a drilling unit or offshore production facility.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Explosives shall be stored in a locked container that
  is accessible only to a qualified person.

<!-- -->

- **[[11.27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.27
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Explosives shall be used, stored and
  controlled by a qualified person.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The qualified person referred to in subsection (1)
  shall make a record of all explosives used or stored by him or removed
  for use.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The record shall be made readily available in any
  form, as determined in consultation with the policy committee or, if
  there is no policy committee, the safety and health committee or the
  safety and health representative, and shall contain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of explosives used, stored or
    removed for use;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date of use, storage or removal;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} particulars of the use of the explosive; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name of the qualified person who made the
    record.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-141, s. 42
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.27-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 11.27"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Radiation Emitting Devices]{.HTitleText3} {#h-909018 .Subheading}

- **[[11.28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.28
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where a device that is capable of
  producing and emitting energy in the form of electromagnetic waves or
  accoustical waves is used in a work place, the employer shall, if the
  device is referred to in subsection (2), adopt and implement the
  applicable safety code of the Bureau of Radiation and Medical Devices
  of the Department of National Health and Welfare as specified in that
  subsection.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), the applicable
  safety code is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of radiofrequency and microwave devices
    in the frequency range 10 MHz-300 GHz, Safety Code --- 6, dated
    February 1979;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of X-ray equipment in medical diagnosis,
    Safety Code --- 20A, dated 1981;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of baggage inspection X-ray equipment,
    Safety Code --- 21, dated May 1978;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in respect of dental X-ray equipment, Safety Code
    --- 22, dated 1981;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in respect of ultrasound, Safety Code --- 23, dated
    1980 and Safety Code --- 24, dated 1980; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in respect of short-wave diathermy, Safety Code ---
    25, dated 1983.

### [DIVISION II]{.HLabel2}[Hazardous Substances Other than Hazardous Products]{.HTitleText2} {#h-909028 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
- SOR/2016-141, s. 53

\]
:::

#### [Asbestos Exposure Management Program]{.HTitleText3} {#h-909031 .Subheading}

##### [Asbestos-containing Material]{.HTitleText4} {#h-909032 .Subheading}

- **[[11.28.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.28.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If asbestos-containing material is
  present in a work place and there is the potential for a release of
  asbestos fibres or employee exposure to asbestos fibres, an employer
  shall ensure that the qualified person who is carrying out a hazard
  investigation under section 11.3 takes into consideration the type of
  asbestos, the condition of the asbestos-containing material, the
  friability of the asbestos-containing material, the accessibility to
  and likelihood of damage to the asbestos-containing material and the
  potential for the release of asbestos fibres or employee exposure to
  asbestos fibres.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} At the completion of an investigation carried out
  under section 11.3, the employer shall ensure that a readily available
  record of the location, friability and condition of the
  asbestos-containing material and the type of asbestos contained in the
  asbestos-containing material is kept and maintained for examination by
  employees and is in any form as determined in consultation with the
  policy committee or, if there is no policy committee, the safety and
  health committee or the safety and health representative.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 12
:::

##### [Asbestos Exposure Control Plan]{.HTitleText4} {#h-909038 .Subheading}

**[[11.28.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.28.2 .sectionLabel}** Before
undertaking any work activity that involves asbestos-containing
material, an employer shall, in consultation with the policy committee
or, if there is no policy committee, the safety and health committee or
the safety and health representative, develop, implement and administer
an asbestos exposure control plan that requires the employer to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} ensure that a hazard investigation under section 11.3
  has been carried out by a qualified person and, in the event that
  there is a change in the work activity, review any report that was
  prepared as a result of the investigation and, if necessary, have a
  qualified person carry out another investigation;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} ensure that a qualified person classifies the work
  activity as a low-risk activity, moderate-risk activity or high-risk
  activity;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} ensure that all asbestos-containing material present
  in the work place that is exposed or that will be disturbed is
  identified by signs and labels or by any other effective manner;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} ensure that all friable asbestos-containing material
  present in the work place is controlled by removal, enclosure or
  encapsulation or by any other effective manner to prevent employee
  exposure to asbestos;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} ensure that procedures and control measures for
  moderate-risk activities and high-risk activities are developed and
  implemented; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} develop and implement an employee education and
  training program that is specific to asbestos-containing material.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 12
:::

**[[11.28.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.28.3 .sectionLabel}** If an employee
who is undertaking automotive service procedures may be exposed to
asbestos from friction material or dust arising from that material, an
employer shall ensure that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the use of compressed air, brushes or similar means
  to dry-remove friction material dust from automotive assemblies is
  prohibited; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} signs to advise employees of the hazards and required
  precautions are posted in service work areas where friction material
  is handled or dust arising from that material is generated.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 12
:::

##### [Asbestos Dust, Waste and Debris Removal]{.HTitleText4} {#h-909053 .Subheading}

- **[[11.28.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.28.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} During any work activities that
  involve friable asbestos-containing materials, an employer shall
  ensure that the following activities are carried out frequently and at
  regular intervals as determined by a qualified person, at the end of
  each work shift and immediately after the work activity is completed:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all asbestos dust, waste and debris are removed by
    vacuuming with a vacuum cleaner that is equipped with a HEPA filter,
    damp-mopping or wet-sweeping the area that is contaminated with the
    asbestos dust, waste or debris; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any drop sheets that are contaminated with asbestos
    dust, waste or debris are wetted.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} All asbestos dust, waste or debris and any drop
  sheets that are contaminated with asbestos dust, waste or debris shall
  be placed in a container referred to in section 11.28.11.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 12
:::

**[[11.28.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.28.5 .sectionLabel}** If a glove bag
is used for the removal of asbestos insulation from pipes, ducts and
similar structures, an employer shall ensure that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the glove bag is sealed to prevent the release of
  asbestos fibres into the work area;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the glove bag is inspected for damage or defects
  immediately before it is attached to the pipe, duct or similar
  structure and at regular intervals during its use;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} all waste from asbestos-containing material that is
  on surfaces is washed to the bottom of the glove bag and all exposed
  asbestos-containing material is encapsulated when it is inside the
  glove bag;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the glove bag is evacuated using a vacuum cleaner
  that is equipped with a HEPA filter to remove the air inside the bag
  prior to the removal of the glove bag; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} after the glove bag is removed, all exposed surfaces
  are cleaned with a damp cloth and a vacuum cleaner that is equipped
  with a HEPA filter.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 12
:::

##### [Decontamination]{.HTitleText4} {#h-909069 .Subheading}

- **[[11.28.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.28.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before leaving a work area that is
  contaminated with asbestos-containing material, an employee shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if their protective clothing is to be reused,
    decontaminate the clothing with a damp cloth or a vacuum cleaner
    that is equipped with a HEPA filter before taking the clothing off;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if their protective clothing is not to be reused,
    place the clothing in a container referred to in section 11.28.11.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An employer shall provide employees with a facility
  reserved for washing their hands and face, and employees shall wash
  their hands and face using that facility before leaving a work area
  that is contaminated with asbestos-containing material.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 12
:::

**[[11.28.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.28.7 .sectionLabel}** As soon as
practicable after any work activity that involves asbestos-containing
material is completed, an employee shall clean reusable tools,
equipment, rigid barriers and portable enclosures that are contaminated
with asbestos with a damp cloth or a vacuum cleaner that is equipped
with a HEPA filter.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 12
:::

##### [Air Sampling]{.HTitleText4} {#h-909080 .Subheading}

- **[[11.28.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.28.8
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An employer shall ensure that a
  qualified person takes air samples to test for airborne asbestos
  fibres

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the vicinity of the containment system during
    any work activity that involves asbestos-containing material and, in
    the case of a work activity that lasts longer than 24 hours, at
    least daily;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the clean room during removal and clean-up
    operations and, in the case of removal and clean-up operations that
    last longer than 24 hours, at least daily; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in contaminated areas that are inside the
    containment system as necessary during removal and clean-up
    operations.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The employer shall ensure that the following air
  samples are taken:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} two samples for every area in an enclosure that is
    10 m^2^ or less;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} three samples for every area in an enclosure that
    is more than 10 m^2^ and not more than 500 m^2^; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} five samples for every area in an enclosure that is
    more than 500 m^2^.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Within 24 hours after obtaining the air sampling test
  results, the employer shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} post a copy of the results in a conspicuous place
    in the work place; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} make the results available to the policy committee,
    if any, the safety and health committee and the safety and health
    representative.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 12
:::

##### [Clearance Air Sampling]{.HTitleText4} {#h-909095 .Subheading}

- **[[11.28.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.28.9
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before dismantling a containment
  system and after all asbestos dust, waste and debris have been cleaned
  up, removed or encapsulated, an employer shall ensure that clearance
  air samples are taken inside the enclosure and that the concentration
  of airborne asbestos fibres is determined in accordance with Method
  7400 set out in the document entitled NIOSH Manual of Analytical
  Methods, published by the National Institute for Occupational Safety
  and Health, as amended from time to time, or in accordance with a
  scientifically proven method used to collect and analyze a
  representative sample of airborne asbestos fibres.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} When conducting clearance air sampling, the employer
  shall ensure that forced air is used inside the enclosure to dislodge
  any asbestos fibres from all surfaces and keep them airborne.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Clearance air sampling shall be taken until the
  concentrations of airborne asbestos fibres do not exceed the values
  referred to in subsection 11.23(1.1).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 12
:::

**[[11.28.10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.28.10 .sectionLabel}** Within 24
hours after obtaining the clearance air sampling test results, the
employer shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} post a copy of the results in a conspicuous place in
  the work place; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} make the results available to the policy committee,
  if any, the safety and health committee and the safety and health
  representative, and provide a copy of the results to the Head of
  Compliance and Enforcement.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 12
- [SOR/2021-118, s. 9](#1295161-1312181){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1295161-1312181.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.28.10-20170620.html "Link to previous version of section 11.28.10"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Containers for Asbestos Dust, Waste and Debris]{.HTitleText4} {#h-909107 .Subheading}

**[[11.28.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.28.11 .sectionLabel}** Containers
for the containment of asbestos dust, waste and debris and
asbestos-containing material shall be

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} dust-tight;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} suitable to contain asbestos dust, waste or debris;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} impervious to asbestos;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} identified as containing asbestos dust, waste or
  debris;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} cleaned with a damp cloth or a vacuum cleaner that is
  equipped with a HEPA filter immediately before being removed from the
  work area; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} removed from the work place frequently and at regular
  intervals as determined by a qualified person.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 12
:::

#### [Identification]{.HTitleText3} {#h-909117 .Subheading}

**[[11.29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.29 .sectionLabel}** Every container of
a hazardous substance, other than a hazardous product, that is stored,
handled or used in the work place shall be labelled in a manner that
discloses clearly

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the substance; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the hazardous properties of the substance.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 12
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
- SOR/2016-141, s. 53
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.29-20140529.html "Link to previous version of section 11.29"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[11.30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.30 .sectionLabel}** If a safety data
sheet in respect of a hazardous substance, other than a hazardous
product, that is stored, handled or used in the work place may be
obtained from the supplier of the hazardous substance, the employer
shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} obtain a copy of the safety data sheet; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} keep a copy of the safety data sheet readily
  available for examination by employees in any form, as determined in
  consultation with the policy committee or, if there is no policy
  committee, the safety and health committee or the safety and health
  representative.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 12
- SOR/2014-141, s. 14(F)
- SOR/2016-141, s. 43
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.30-20140529.html "Link to previous version of section 11.30"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION III]{.HLabel2}[Hazardous Products]{.HTitleText2} {#h-909128 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2016-141, s. 53

\]
:::

#### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-909131 .Subheading}

**[[11.31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.31 .sectionLabel}** In this Division,

[[bulk shipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bulk shipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    subsection 5.5(1) of the [Hazardous Products
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2015-17); ([expédition en
    vrac]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any package or receptacle,
    including a bag, barrel, bottle, box, can, cylinder, drum and
    storage tank; ([contenant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[fugitive emission]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fugitive emission]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a hazardous product
    in gas, liquid, solid, vapour, fume, mist, fog or dust form that
    escapes from processing equipment, from control emission equipment
    or from a product into the work place; ([émission
    fugitive]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[hazardous waste]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[hazardous waste]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a hazardous product
    that is acquired or generated for recycling or recovery or is
    intended for disposal; ([résidu dangereux]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[laboratory sample]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[laboratory sample]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    subsection 5(1) of the [Hazardous Products
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2015-17); ([échantillon pour
    laboratoire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[manufactured article]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[manufactured article]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/2016-141, s. 44\]]{.Repealed}

[[readily available]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[readily available]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/2016-141, s. 44\]]{.Repealed}

[[risk phrase]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[risk phrase]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a hazardous
    product, a statement identifying a hazard that may arise from the
    use of or exposure to the hazardous product; ([mention de
    risque]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[sale]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[sale]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes offer for sale, expose for
    sale and distribute; ([vente]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[significant new data]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[significant new data]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
    in subsection 5.12(1) of the [Hazardous Products
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2015-17); ([nouvelles données
    importantes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[supplier label]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[supplier label]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a label prepared by a
    supplier that discloses any information elements required by the
    [Hazardous Products Act](/eng/acts/H-3); ([étiquette du
    fournisseur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[supplier material safety data sheet]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[supplier material safety data
    sheet]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2016-141, s.
    44\]]{.Repealed}

[[supplier safety data sheet]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[supplier safety data sheet]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a safety
    data sheet prepared by a supplier that discloses any information
    elements required by the [Hazardous Products Act](/eng/acts/H-3);
    ([fiche de données de sécurité du fournisseur]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[work place label]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[work place label]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a label prepared by
    an employer in accordance with this Division; ([étiquette du lieu de
    travail]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[work place material safety data sheet]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[work place material safety data
    sheet]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2016-141, s.
    44\]]{.Repealed}

[[work place safety data sheet]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[work place safety data sheet]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a safety
    data sheet prepared by an employer in accordance with subsection
    11.35(1) or (2). ([fiche de données de sécurité du lieu de
    travail]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 12
- SOR/2016-141, ss. 44, 53
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.31-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 11.31"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Application]{.HTitleText3} {#h-909151 .Subheading}

- **[[11.32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.32
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This Division does not apply in
  respect of any

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} tobacco or a tobacco product as defined in section
    2 of the [Tobacco and Vaping Products Act](/eng/acts/T-11.5);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} manufactured article as defined in section 2 of the
    [Hazardous Products Act](/eng/acts/H-3); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} wood or a product made of wood.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} This Division, other than section 11.45, does not
  apply in respect of hazardous waste.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 12
- SOR/2016-141, s. 45
- 2018, c. 9, s. 77
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.32-20160614.html "Link to previous version of section 11.32"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Safety Data Sheets and Labels in Respect of Certain Hazardous Products]{.HTitleText3} {#h-909160 .Subheading}

**[[11.33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.33 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section
11.44, every employer shall implement the provisions of sections 11.29
and 11.30 in respect of a hazardous product and may, in so doing,
replace the generic name of the product with the brand name, chemical
name, common name or trade name, if the hazardous product

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is present in the work place;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} was received from a supplier; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} is one of the following:

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a hazardous product, other than wood or a product
    made of wood, that is listed in Schedule 1 to the [Hazardous
    Products Act](/eng/acts/H-3),

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a nuclear substance as defined in section 2 of the
    [Nuclear Safety and Control Act](/eng/acts/N-28.3), that is
    radioactive.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-199, s. 12
- SOR/2016-141, s. 46
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.33-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 11.33"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Supplier Safety Data Sheets]{.HTitleText3} {#h-909169 .Subheading}

- **[[11.34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.34
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a hazardous product, other than a
  hazardous product referred to in paragraph 11.33(c), is received in
  the work place by an employer, the employer shall, without delay,
  obtain a supplier safety data sheet in respect of the hazardous
  product from the supplier, unless the employer is already in
